- **blackbox-covering**: 独立した因子が多く全組合せが爆発するとき、因子の被覆や均等割付でケース数を縮約するブラックボックス技法群。 test-catalog の手法カタログの一部。ペアワイズ(Pairwise/All-pairs、全ペアを最低1回被覆)、 直交表(Orthogonal Array、全ペアを同回数で均等割付し主効果まで読む)、 T-way テスト(t個組の高次組合せと制約付き covering array) を検証したい、または割り当てたいときに使う。通常は test-catalog スキルの索引経由で 手法が選定された後にこのスキルを直接参照する。 ([Hakkadaikon/hymme](https://github.com/Hakkadaikon/hymme/tree/HEAD/skills/blackbox-covering))

- **blindspot**: Surface the USER's unknown-unknowns before they work in unfamiliar territory, an unfamiliar codebase area OR an unfamiliar domain vocabulary, and coach a sharper prompt. Use when: 'what am I missing', 'find my blindspots', 'what do I not know here', 'sharpen this prompt', 'I am new to this area', or about to work in an unfamiliar area and the goal is a better prompt, not the codebase handoff art… ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/discovery/skills/blindspot) · ★17)
- **blindspot**: Peripheral vision audit — find what the author has become blind to in their results. Run in a FRESH TERMINAL. ([saptarsibhowmick/claude-workflow-for-econ-phd](https://github.com/saptarsibhowmick/claude-workflow-for-econ-phd/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/blindspot) · ★10)
- **blindspot**: Read-only reconnaissance pass over an unfamiliar codebase, system, or feature area to surface hidden risks before any code is written. Use when starting work in unfamiliar territory, before a risky change, or when the user says "blindspot pass", "what am I missing", or "scan for risks". ([hiendinhngoc/unknowns](https://github.com/hiendinhngoc/unknowns/tree/HEAD/skills/blindspot) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **blindspot**: Find the unknowns before they find the work — a read-only territory pass that surfaces the questions nobody knew to ask, judgment taught before a choice is extracted, and a quiz that catches the user's map up with what actually changed. Use when work enters territory the user calls unfamiliar, when the user is confirming a choice they can't evaluate, or when the user asks what they're missing or wants to be quizzed before accepting built work. ([donald-ada/workinggenius](https://github.com/donald-ada/workinggenius/tree/HEAD/skills/blindspot) · ★6)
- **blindspot**: Turn an idea — or a half-planned project already underway — into a buildable blueprint, by interviewing the user one question at a time until the unknowns run out, then deep-reading and verifying real open-source components for each part. Use this whenever someone describes something they want to build but has not specified it ('I want to build an app that…', 'I have an idea for…', 'help me plan/spec/scope this', 'turn my idea into a plan'), whenever they ask for a PRD, a spec, a product requirements document or a build plan, AND equally whenever someone says an existing project feels under-p… ([IdanTayree/Blindspot](https://github.com/IdanTayree/Blindspot/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★5)
- **blindspot**: Inspect unfamiliar or high-stakes code before edits, including auth, billing, and migrations. ([ucsandman/claude-skills](https://github.com/ucsandman/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/blindspot) · ★5)
- **blindspot**: Map the user's unknown-unknowns before work starts — a read-only blindspot pass over an unfamiliar codebase area, domain, or tool. Use when the user says "blindspot pass" or "unknown unknowns", discloses that the area is new or unfamiliar to them, or asks to be taught a domain so they can prompt better. ([Gharib89/crm](https://github.com/Gharib89/crm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/blindspot) · ★3)
- **blindspot**: Mirrors patterns in the user's own thinking, decisions, and work across sessions — friction, progress, early signs of strain or fatigue, and heavier mental-health signals — each backed by evidence from the user's own words. Invoke manually with /blindspot, ideally at the end of a work session. Not a therapist, coach, or diagnostician: it observes, names patterns, and offers light optional nudges (a break, water, sleep); the user decides what they mean. ([isas1/blindspot](https://github.com/isas1/blindspot/tree/HEAD/skills/blindspot) · ★2)
- **blindspot**: Pre-mortem pass before infra, deploy, data-model, or unfamiliar-territory work. Surfaces unknown unknowns by cross-referencing the task against past gotchas in memory and the actual repo surfaces it touches. - **blindspot**: Blind-spot pass — investigate unknown unknowns before implementation and turn them into a better prompt. - **blindspot**: 設計文書・実装計画の確定前に、暗黙知と未認知の仕様穴を洗い出すメタ認知チェック。現在地の棚卸しと、プロジェクト文脈を持たない Haiku の初見リードを突き合わせ、「書き漏らした暗黙知」と「未認知の穴」を分離する。設計や計画がまとまったタイミング、「仕様レビューして」「この設計でどう？」等の発話で使う。 ([Kaito-Tsuruishi/blindspot](https://github.com/Kaito-Tsuruishi/blindspot/tree/HEAD/skills/blindspot) · ★1)
